  264 | begin
  274 |   csr_write: process(rstn_i, clk_i)
  275 |   begin
  276 |     if (rstn_i = '0') then
  277 |       csr_frm    <= (others => '0');
  278 |       csr_fflags <= (others => '0');
  279 |     elsif rising_edge(clk_i) then
  280 |       if (csr_we_i = '1') then
  282 |         if (csr_addr_i = csr_fflags_c(1 downto 0)) then
  283 |           csr_fflags <= csr_wdata_i(4 downto 0);
  284 |         end if;
  286 |         if (csr_addr_i = csr_frm_c(1 downto 0)) then
  287 |           csr_frm <= csr_wdata_i(2 downto 0);
  288 |         end if;
  290 |         if (csr_addr_i = csr_fcsr_c(1 downto 0)) then
  291 |           csr_frm    <= csr_wdata_i(7 downto 5);
  292 |           csr_fflags <= csr_wdata_i(4 downto 0);
  293 |         end if;
  294 |       else
  295 |         csr_fflags <= csr_fflags or fflags;
  296 |       end if;
  297 |     end if;
  298 |   end process csr_write;
  301 |   csr_read: process(csr_addr_i, csr_fflags, csr_frm)
  302 |   begin
  303 |     csr_rdata_o <= (others => '0');
  304 |     case csr_addr_i is
  305 |       when "01"   => csr_rdata_o(4 downto 0) <= csr_fflags;
  306 |       when "10"   => csr_rdata_o(2 downto 0) <= csr_frm;
  307 |       when "11"   => csr_rdata_o(7 downto 0) <= csr_frm & csr_fflags;
  308 |       when others => NULL;
  309 |     end case;
  310 |   end process csr_read;
  316 |   cmd.instr_class  <= '1' when (ctrl_i.ir_funct12(11 downto 7) = "11100") and (ctrl_i.ir_funct3 = "001")               else '0';
  317 |   cmd.instr_comp   <= '1' when (ctrl_i.ir_funct12(11 downto 7) = "10100") and (ctrl_i.ir_funct3(2) = '0')              else '0';
  318 |   cmd.instr_i2f    <= '1' when (ctrl_i.ir_funct12(11 downto 7) = "11010") and (ctrl_i.ir_funct12(4 downto 1) = "0000") else '0';
  319 |   cmd.instr_f2i    <= '1' when (ctrl_i.ir_funct12(11 downto 7) = "11000") and (ctrl_i.ir_funct12(4 downto 1) = "0000") else '0';
  320 |   cmd.instr_sgnj   <= '1' when (ctrl_i.ir_funct12(11 downto 7) = "00100") and (ctrl_i.ir_funct3(2) = '0')              else '0';
  321 |   cmd.instr_minmax <= '1' when (ctrl_i.ir_funct12(11 downto 7) = "00101") and (ctrl_i.ir_funct3(2 downto 1) = "00")    else '0';
  322 |   cmd.instr_addsub <= '1' when (ctrl_i.ir_funct12(11 downto 8) = "0000")                                               else '0';
  323 |   cmd.instr_mul    <= '1' when (ctrl_i.ir_funct12(11 downto 7) = "00010")                                              else '0';
  326 |   cmd.valid <= '1' when (ctrl_i.ir_funct12(6 downto 5) = "00") and
  327 |                         ((cmd.instr_class  = '1') or (cmd.instr_comp   = '1') or
  328 |                          (cmd.instr_i2f    = '1') or (cmd.instr_f2i    = '1') or
  329 |                          (cmd.instr_sgnj   = '1') or (cmd.instr_minmax = '1') or
  330 |                          (cmd.instr_addsub = '1') or (cmd.instr_mul    = '1')) else '0';
  333 |   cmd.funct <= op_mul_c    when (cmd.instr_mul    = '1') else
  334 |                op_addsub_c when (cmd.instr_addsub = '1') else
  335 |                op_minmax_c when (cmd.instr_minmax = '1') else
  336 |                op_sgnj_c   when (cmd.instr_sgnj   = '1') else
  337 |                op_f2i_c    when (cmd.instr_f2i    = '1') else
  338 |                op_i2f_c    when (cmd.instr_i2f    = '1') else
  339 |                op_comp_c   when (cmd.instr_comp   = '1') else
  340 |                op_class_c;
  347 |   op_data(0)(31)           <= rs1_i(31);
  348 |   op_data(0)(30 downto 23) <= rs1_i(30 downto 23);
  349 |   op_data(0)(22 downto 0)  <= (others => '0') when (rs1_i(30 downto 23) = "00000000") else rs1_i(22 downto 0);
  351 |   op_data(1)(31)           <= rs2_i(31);
  352 |   op_data(1)(30 downto 23) <= rs2_i(30 downto 23);
  353 |   op_data(1)(22 downto 0)  <= (others => '0') when (rs2_i(30 downto 23) = "00000000") else rs2_i(22 downto 0);
  358 |   number_classifier: process(op_data, rs1_i, rs2_i)
  359 |     variable op_m_all_zero_v, op_e_all_zero_v, op_e_all_one_v       : std_ulogic;
  360 |     variable op_is_zero_v, op_is_inf_v, op_is_denorm_v, op_is_nan_v : std_ulogic;
  361 |   begin
  362 |     for i in 0 to 1 loop
  364 |       op_m_all_zero_v := not or_reduce_f(op_data(i)(22 downto 0));
  365 |       op_e_all_zero_v := not or_reduce_f(op_data(i)(30 downto 23));
  366 |       op_e_all_one_v  :=    and_reduce_f(op_data(i)(30 downto 23));
  369 |       op_is_zero_v := op_e_all_zero_v and op_m_all_zero_v;
  370 |       op_is_inf_v  := op_e_all_one_v  and op_m_all_zero_v;
  373 |       if (i = 0) then
  374 |         op_is_denorm_v := or_reduce_f(rs1_i(22 downto 0)) and op_e_all_zero_v;
  375 |       end if;
  376 |       if (i = 1) then
  377 |         op_is_denorm_v := or_reduce_f(rs2_i(22 downto 0)) and op_e_all_zero_v;
  378 |       end if;
  383 |       op_is_nan_v := op_e_all_one_v and (not op_m_all_zero_v);
  386 |       op_class(i)(fp_class_neg_inf_c)    <= op_data(i)(31) and op_is_inf_v;
  387 |       op_class(i)(fp_class_neg_norm_c)   <= op_data(i)(31) and (not op_is_denorm_v) and (not op_is_nan_v) and (not op_is_inf_v) and (not op_is_zero_v);
  388 |       op_class(i)(fp_class_neg_denorm_c) <= op_data(i)(31) and op_is_denorm_v;
  389 |       op_class(i)(fp_class_neg_zero_c)   <= op_data(i)(31) and op_is_zero_v and (not op_is_denorm_v);
  390 |       op_class(i)(fp_class_pos_zero_c)   <= (not op_data(i)(31)) and op_is_zero_v and (not op_is_denorm_v);
  391 |       op_class(i)(fp_class_pos_denorm_c) <= (not op_data(i)(31)) and op_is_denorm_v;
  392 |       op_class(i)(fp_class_pos_norm_c)   <= (not op_data(i)(31)) and (not op_is_denorm_v) and (not op_is_nan_v) and (not op_is_inf_v) and (not op_is_zero_v);
  393 |       op_class(i)(fp_class_pos_inf_c)    <= (not op_data(i)(31)) and op_is_inf_v;
  394 |       op_class(i)(fp_class_snan_c)       <= op_is_nan_v and (not op_data(i)(22));
  395 |       op_class(i)(fp_class_qnan_c)       <= op_is_nan_v and (    op_data(i)(22));
  396 |     end loop;
  397 |   end process number_classifier;
  402 |   control_engine_fsm: process(rstn_i, clk_i)
  403 |   begin
  404 |     if (rstn_i = '0') then
  405 |       ctrl_engine.state      <= S_IDLE;
  406 |       ctrl_engine.valid      <= '0';
  407 |       ctrl_engine.start      <= '0';
  408 |       fpu_operands.frm       <= (others => '0');
  409 |       fpu_operands.rs1       <= (others => '0');
  410 |       fpu_operands.rs1_class <= (others => '0');
  411 |       fpu_operands.rs2       <= (others => '0');
  412 |       fpu_operands.rs2_class <= (others => '0');
  413 |       funct_ff               <= (others => '0');
  414 |       cmp_ff                 <= (others => '0');
  415 |     elsif rising_edge(clk_i) then
  417 |       ctrl_engine.valid <= '0';
  418 |       ctrl_engine.start <= '0';
  421 |       case ctrl_engine.state is
  423 |         when S_IDLE =>
  425 |           funct_ff <= cmd.funct;
  426 |           cmp_ff   <= cmp_i;
  429 |           if (ctrl_i.ir_funct3 = "111") then
  430 |             fpu_operands.frm <= csr_frm(2 downto 0);
  431 |           else
  432 |             fpu_operands.frm <= ctrl_i.ir_funct3(2 downto 0);
  433 |           end if;
  435 |           if (ctrl_i.alu_cp_fpu = '1') and (cmd.valid = '1') then
  437 |             fpu_operands.rs1       <= op_data(0);
  438 |             fpu_operands.rs1_class <= op_class(0);
  439 |             fpu_operands.rs2       <= op_data(1);
  440 |             fpu_operands.rs2_class <= op_class(1);
  442 |             ctrl_engine.start <= '1';
  443 |             ctrl_engine.state <= S_BUSY;
  444 |           end if;
  446 |         when S_BUSY =>
  448 |           if (fu_core_done = '1') or (ctrl_i.cpu_trap = '1') then
  449 |             ctrl_engine.valid <= '1';
  450 |             ctrl_engine.state <= S_IDLE;
  451 |           end if;
  453 |         when others =>
  455 |           ctrl_engine.state <= S_IDLE;
  457 |       end case;
  458 |     end if;
  459 |   end process control_engine_fsm;
  462 |   valid_o <= ctrl_engine.valid;
  467 |   fu_classify.start    <= ctrl_engine.start and cmd.instr_class;
  468 |   fu_compare.start     <= ctrl_engine.start and cmd.instr_comp;
  469 |   fu_sign_inject.start <= ctrl_engine.start and cmd.instr_sgnj;
  470 |   fu_min_max.start     <= ctrl_engine.start and cmd.instr_minmax;
  471 |   fu_conv_i2f.start    <= ctrl_engine.start and cmd.instr_i2f;
  472 |   fu_conv_f2i.start    <= ctrl_engine.start and cmd.instr_f2i;
  473 |   fu_addsub.start      <= ctrl_engine.start and cmd.instr_addsub;
  474 |   fu_mul.start         <= ctrl_engine.start and cmd.instr_mul;
  483 |   fu_classify.flags <= (others => '0');
  484 |   fu_classify.result(31 downto 10) <= (others => '0');
  485 |   fu_classify.result(9 downto 0) <= fpu_operands.rs1_class;
  486 |   fu_classify.done <= fu_classify.start;
  491 |   float_comparator: process(rstn_i, clk_i)
  492 |     variable cond_v : std_ulogic_vector(1 downto 0);
  493 |   begin
  494 |     if (rstn_i = '0') then
  495 |       comp_equal_ff   <= '0';
  496 |       comp_less_ff    <= '0';
  497 |       fu_compare.done <= '0';
  498 |       fu_min_max.done <= '0';
  499 |     elsif rising_edge(clk_i) then
  502 |       if (not FPU_SUBNORMAL_SUPPORT) then
  503 |         if ((fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_pos_inf_c)     = '1') and (fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_pos_inf_c)   = '1')) or
  504 |            ((fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_neg_inf_c)     = '1') and (fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_neg_inf_c)   = '1')) or
  505 |            (((fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_pos_zero_c)   = '1') or (fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_neg_zero_c)   = '1'))  and
  506 |             ((fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_pos_zero_c)   = '1') or (fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_neg_zero_c)   = '1')))  or
  507 |            (((fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_pos_denorm_c) = '1') or (fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_neg_denorm_c) = '1'))  and
  508 |             ((fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_pos_zero_c)   = '1') or (fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_neg_zero_c)   = '1'))) or
  509 |            (((fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_pos_denorm_c) = '1') or (fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_neg_denorm_c) = '1'))  and
  510 |             ((fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_pos_denorm_c) = '1') or (fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_neg_denorm_c) = '1'))) or
  511 |            (((fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_pos_zero_c)   = '1') or (fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_neg_zero_c)   = '1'))  and
  512 |             ((fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_pos_denorm_c) = '1') or (fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_neg_denorm_c) = '1'))) or
  513 |            (cmp_ff(cmp_equal_c) = '1') then
  514 |           comp_equal_ff <= '1';
  515 |         else
  516 |           comp_equal_ff <= '0';
  517 |         end if;
  518 |       else
  519 |         if ((fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_pos_inf_c)   = '1') and (fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_pos_inf_c) = '1')) or
  520 |            ((fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_neg_inf_c)   = '1') and (fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_neg_inf_c) = '1')) or
  521 |            (((fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_pos_zero_c) = '1') or (fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_neg_zero_c) = '1')) and
  522 |             ((fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_pos_zero_c) = '1') or (fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_neg_zero_c) = '1'))) or
  523 |            (cmp_ff(cmp_equal_c) = '1') then
  524 |           comp_equal_ff <= '1';
  525 |         else
  526 |           comp_equal_ff <= '0';
  527 |         end if;
  528 |       end if;
  532 |       if (not FPU_SUBNORMAL_SUPPORT) then
  533 |         if ((fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_pos_inf_c)     = '1') and (fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_pos_inf_c)   = '1'))  or
  534 |            ((fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_neg_inf_c)     = '1') and (fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_neg_inf_c)   = '1'))  or
  535 |            (((fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_pos_zero_c)   = '1') or (fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_neg_zero_c)   = '1'))  and
  536 |             ((fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_pos_zero_c)   = '1') or (fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_neg_zero_c)   = '1'))) or
  537 |            (((fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_pos_denorm_c) = '1') or (fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_neg_denorm_c) = '1'))  and
  538 |             ((fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_pos_zero_c)   = '1') or (fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_neg_zero_c)   = '1'))) or
  539 |            (((fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_pos_denorm_c) = '1') or (fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_neg_denorm_c) = '1'))  and
  540 |             ((fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_pos_denorm_c) = '1') or (fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_neg_denorm_c) = '1'))) or
  541 |            (((fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_pos_zero_c)   = '1') or (fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_neg_zero_c)   = '1'))  and
  542 |             ((fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_pos_denorm_c) = '1') or (fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_neg_denorm_c) = '1'))) then
  543 |           comp_less_ff <= '0';
  544 |         else
  545 |           cond_v := fpu_operands.rs1(31) & fpu_operands.rs2(31);
  546 |           case cond_v is
  547 |             when "10"   => comp_less_ff <= '1';
  548 |             when "01"   => comp_less_ff <= '0';
  549 |             when "00"   => comp_less_ff <= cmp_ff(cmp_less_c);
  552 |             when "11"   => comp_less_ff <= (not cmp_ff(cmp_less_c)) and (not cmp_ff(cmp_equal_c));
  553 |             when others => comp_less_ff <= '0';
  554 |           end case;
  555 |         end if;
  556 |       else
  557 |         if ((fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_pos_inf_c)  = '1') and (fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_pos_inf_c) = '1')) or
  558 |            ((fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_neg_inf_c)  = '1') and (fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_neg_inf_c) = '1')) or
  559 |            (((fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_pos_zero_c) = '1') or (fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_neg_zero_c) = '1')) and
  560 |             ((fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_pos_zero_c) = '1') or (fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_neg_zero_c) = '1'))) then
  561 |           comp_less_ff <= '0';
  562 |         else
  563 |           cond_v := fpu_operands.rs1(31) & fpu_operands.rs2(31);
  564 |           case cond_v is
  565 |             when "10"   => comp_less_ff <= '1';
  566 |             when "01"   => comp_less_ff <= '0';
  567 |             when "00"   => comp_less_ff <= cmp_ff(cmp_less_c);
  570 |             when "11"   => comp_less_ff <= not cmp_ff(cmp_less_c) and (not cmp_ff(cmp_equal_c));
  571 |             when others => comp_less_ff <= '0';
  572 |           end case;
  573 |         end if;
  574 |       end if;
  577 |       fu_compare.done <= fu_compare.start;
  578 |       fu_min_max.done <= fu_min_max.start;
  579 |     end if;
  580 |   end process float_comparator;
  585 |   float_comparison: process(fpu_operands, ctrl_i, comp_equal_ff, comp_less_ff)
  586 |     variable snan_v : std_ulogic;
  587 |     variable qnan_v : std_ulogic;
  588 |   begin
  590 |     snan_v := fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_snan_c) or fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_snan_c);
  591 |     qnan_v := fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_qnan_c) or fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_qnan_c);
  595 |     fu_compare.flags <= (others => '0');
  597 |     fu_compare.result <= (others => '0');
  598 |     case ctrl_i.ir_funct3(1 downto 0) is
  599 |       when "00" =>
  600 |         fu_compare.result(0) <= (comp_less_ff or comp_equal_ff) and (not (snan_v or qnan_v));
  602 |         if ((snan_v or qnan_v) = '1') then
  603 |           fu_compare.flags(fp_exc_nv_c) <= '1';
  604 |         end if;
  605 |       when "01" =>
  606 |         fu_compare.result(0) <= comp_less_ff and (not (snan_v or qnan_v));
  608 |         if ((snan_v or qnan_v) = '1') then
  609 |           fu_compare.flags(fp_exc_nv_c) <= '1';
  610 |         end if;
  611 |       when "10" =>
  612 |         fu_compare.result(0) <= comp_equal_ff and (not (snan_v or qnan_v));
  615 |         if ((snan_v) = '1') then
  616 |           fu_compare.flags(fp_exc_nv_c) <= '1';
  617 |         end if;
  618 |       when others =>
  619 |         fu_compare.result(0) <= '0';
  620 |     end case;
  621 |   end process float_comparison;
  630 |   min_max_select: process(fpu_operands, comp_less_ff, ctrl_i)
  631 |     variable cond_v : std_ulogic_vector(2 downto 0);
  632 |   begin
  635 |     if (not FPU_SUBNORMAL_SUPPORT) then
  636 |       if (((fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_neg_zero_c)   = '1') and (fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_pos_denorm_c) = '1')) or
  637 |           ((fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_neg_denorm_c) = '1') and (fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_pos_zero_c)   = '1')) or
  638 |           ((fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_neg_denorm_c) = '1') and (fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_pos_denorm_c) = '1')) or
  639 |           ((fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_neg_zero_c)   = '1') and (fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_pos_zero_c)   = '1'))) then
  640 |         cond_v(0) := ctrl_i.ir_funct3(0);
  641 |       elsif (((fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_pos_zero_c)   = '1') and (fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_neg_denorm_c) = '1')) or
  642 |              ((fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_pos_denorm_c) = '1') and (fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_neg_zero_c)   = '1')) or
  643 |              ((fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_pos_denorm_c) = '1') and (fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_neg_denorm_c) = '1')) or
  644 |              ((fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_pos_zero_c)   = '1') and (fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_neg_zero_c)   = '1'))) then
  645 |         cond_v(0) := not ctrl_i.ir_funct3(0);
  646 |       else
  647 |         cond_v(0) := not (comp_less_ff xor ctrl_i.ir_funct3(0));
  648 |       end if;
  649 |     else
  650 |       if ((fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_neg_zero_c) = '1') and (fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_pos_zero_c) = '1')) then
  651 |         cond_v(0) := ctrl_i.ir_funct3(0);
  652 |       elsif ((fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_pos_zero_c) = '1') and (fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_neg_zero_c) = '1')) then
  653 |         cond_v(0) := not ctrl_i.ir_funct3(0);
  654 |       else
  655 |         cond_v(0) := not (comp_less_ff xor ctrl_i.ir_funct3(0));
  656 |       end if;
  657 |     end if;
  660 |     cond_v(2) := fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_snan_c) or fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_qnan_c);
  661 |     cond_v(1) := fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_snan_c) or fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_qnan_c);
  665 |     fu_min_max.flags <= (others => '0');
  667 |     if ((fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_snan_c) or fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_snan_c)) = '1') then
  668 |       fu_min_max.flags(fp_exc_nv_c) <= '1';
  669 |     end if;
  672 |     case cond_v is
  673 |       when "000"         => fu_min_max.result <= fpu_operands.rs1;
  674 |       when "001"         => fu_min_max.result <= fpu_operands.rs2;
  675 |       when "010" | "011" => fu_min_max.result <= fpu_operands.rs1;
  676 |       when "100" | "101" => fu_min_max.result <= fpu_operands.rs2;
  677 |       when others        => fu_min_max.result <= fp_single_qnan_c;
  678 |     end case;
  679 |   end process min_max_select;
  686 |   neorv32_cpu_cp_fpu_f2i_inst: neorv32_cpu_cp_fpu_f2i
  687 |   generic map (
  689 |     FPU_SUBNORMAL_SUPPORT => FPU_SUBNORMAL_SUPPORT
  690 |   )
  691 |   port map (
  693 |     clk_i      => clk_i,
  694 |     rstn_i     => rstn_i,
  695 |     start_i    => fu_conv_f2i.start,
  696 |     abort_i    => ctrl_i.cpu_trap,
  697 |     rmode_i    => fpu_operands.frm,
  698 |     funct_i    => ctrl_i.ir_funct12(0),
  700 |     sign_i     => fpu_operands.rs1(31),
  701 |     exponent_i => fpu_operands.rs1(30 downto 23),
  702 |     mantissa_i => fpu_operands.rs1(22 downto 0),
  703 |     class_i    => fpu_operands.rs1_class,
  705 |     result_o   => fu_conv_f2i.result,
  706 |     flags_o    => fu_conv_f2i.flags,
  707 |     done_o     => fu_conv_f2i.done
  708 |   );
  713 |   sign_injector: process(ctrl_i, fpu_operands, rs1_i)
  714 |   begin
  715 |     case ctrl_i.ir_funct3(1 downto 0) is
  716 |       when "00"   => fu_sign_inject.result(31) <= fpu_operands.rs2(31);
  717 |       when "01"   => fu_sign_inject.result(31) <= not fpu_operands.rs2(31);
  718 |       when "10"   => fu_sign_inject.result(31) <= fpu_operands.rs1(31) xor fpu_operands.rs2(31);
  719 |       when others => fu_sign_inject.result(31) <= fpu_operands.rs2(31);
  720 |     end case;
  723 |     if (not FPU_SUBNORMAL_SUPPORT) then
  724 |       fu_sign_inject.result(30 downto 0) <= rs1_i(30 downto 0);
  725 |     else
  726 |       fu_sign_inject.result(30 downto 0) <= fpu_operands.rs1(30 downto 0);
  727 |     end if;
  728 |     fu_sign_inject.flags <= (others => '0');
  729 |   end process sign_injector;
  732 |   fu_sign_inject.done <= fu_sign_inject.start;
  737 |   convert_i2f: process(rstn_i, clk_i)
  738 |   begin
  741 |     if (rstn_i = '0') then
  742 |       fu_conv_i2f.result <= (others => '0');
  743 |       fu_conv_i2f.sign   <= '0';
  744 |       fu_conv_i2f.done   <= '0';
  745 |     elsif rising_edge(clk_i) then
  746 |       if (ctrl_i.ir_funct12(0) = '0') and (rs1_i(31) = '1') then
  747 |         fu_conv_i2f.result <= std_ulogic_vector(0 - unsigned(rs1_i));
  748 |         fu_conv_i2f.sign   <= rs1_i(31);
  749 |       else
  750 |         fu_conv_i2f.result <= rs1_i;
  751 |         fu_conv_i2f.sign   <= '0';
  752 |       end if;
  753 |       fu_conv_i2f.done <= fu_conv_i2f.start;
  754 |     end if;
  755 |   end process convert_i2f;
  760 |   multiplier_core: process(rstn_i, clk_i)
  761 |   begin
  762 |     if (rstn_i = '0') then
  763 |       multiplier.opa     <= (others => '0');
  764 |       multiplier.opb     <= (others => '0');
  765 |       multiplier.buf_ff  <= (others => '0');
  766 |       multiplier.product <= (others => '0');
  767 |       multiplier.sign    <= '0';
  768 |       multiplier.exp_res <= (others => '0');
  769 |       multiplier.flags   <= (others => '0');
  770 |       multiplier.latency <= (others => '0');
  771 |     elsif rising_edge(clk_i) then
  774 |       if ((fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_pos_zero_c)   or fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_neg_zero_c)   or
  775 |            fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_pos_zero_c)   or fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_neg_zero_c)   or
  776 |            fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_pos_denorm_c) or fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_neg_denorm_c) or
  777 |            fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_pos_denorm_c) or fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_neg_denorm_c)) = '1') then
  778 |         if (multiplier.start = '1') then
  780 |           multiplier.product <= (others => '0');
  781 |           multiplier.exp_res <= (others => '0');
  782 |         end if;
  783 |       else
  784 |         if (multiplier.start = '1') then
  785 |           multiplier.opa <= unsigned('1' & fpu_operands.rs1(22 downto 0));
  786 |           multiplier.opb <= unsigned('1' & fpu_operands.rs2(22 downto 0));
  787 |         end if;
  788 |         multiplier.buf_ff  <= multiplier.opa * multiplier.opb;
  789 |         multiplier.product <= std_ulogic_vector(multiplier.buf_ff(47 downto 0));
  790 |         multiplier.exp_res <= std_ulogic_vector(unsigned('0' & multiplier.exp_sum) - 127);
  791 |       end if;
  792 |       multiplier.sign <= fpu_operands.rs1(31) xor fpu_operands.rs2(31);
  796 |       multiplier.flags(fp_exc_of_c) <= '0';
  797 |       multiplier.flags(fp_exc_uf_c) <= '0';
  798 |       multiplier.flags(fp_exc_nx_c) <= '0';
  802 |       if ((fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_pos_inf_c) or fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_pos_inf_c) or
  803 |            fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_neg_inf_c) or fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_neg_inf_c) or
  804 |            fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_snan_c)    or fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_snan_c)    or
  805 |            fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_qnan_c)    or fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_qnan_c)) = '0') then
  806 |         if (multiplier.exp_res(multiplier.exp_res'left) = '1') then
  807 |           multiplier.flags(fp_exc_of_c) <= '0';
  808 |           multiplier.flags(fp_exc_uf_c) <= '1';
  810 |           multiplier.flags(fp_exc_nx_c) <= '1';
  811 |         elsif (multiplier.exp_res(multiplier.exp_res'left-1) = '1') then
  812 |           multiplier.flags(fp_exc_of_c) <= '1';
  813 |           multiplier.flags(fp_exc_uf_c) <= '0';
  815 |           multiplier.flags(fp_exc_nx_c) <= '1';
  816 |         end if;
  817 |       end if;
  823 |       if (not FPU_SUBNORMAL_SUPPORT) then
  824 |         multiplier.flags(fp_exc_nv_c) <=
  825 |           ((fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_snan_c)       or fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_snan_c))) or
  826 |           ((fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_pos_denorm_c) or fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_neg_denorm_c)) and
  827 |            (fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_pos_inf_c)    or fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_neg_inf_c))) or
  828 |           ((fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_pos_inf_c)    or fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_neg_inf_c)) and
  829 |            (fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_pos_denorm_c) or fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_neg_denorm_c))) or
  830 |           ((fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_pos_zero_c)   or fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_neg_zero_c)) and
  831 |            (fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_pos_inf_c)    or fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_neg_inf_c))) or
  832 |           ((fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_pos_inf_c)    or fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_neg_inf_c)) and
  833 |            (fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_pos_zero_c)   or fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_neg_zero_c)));
  834 |       else
  835 |         multiplier.flags(fp_exc_nv_c) <=
  836 |           ((fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_pos_zero_c) or fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_neg_zero_c)) and
  837 |            (fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_pos_inf_c)  or fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_neg_inf_c))) or
  838 |           ((fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_pos_inf_c)  or fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_neg_inf_c)) and
  839 |            (fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_pos_zero_c) or fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_neg_zero_c)));
  840 |       end if;
  843 |       multiplier.flags(fp_exc_dz_c) <= '0';
  846 |       multiplier.latency <= multiplier.latency(multiplier.latency'left-1 downto 0) & multiplier.start;
  847 |     end if;
  848 |   end process multiplier_core;
  851 |   multiplier.exp_sum <= std_ulogic_vector(unsigned('0' & fpu_operands.rs1(30 downto 23)) + unsigned('0' & fpu_operands.rs2(30 downto 23)));
  854 |   multiplier.start <= fu_mul.start;
  855 |   multiplier.done  <= multiplier.latency(multiplier.latency'left);
  856 |   fu_mul.done      <= multiplier.done;
  860 |   multiplier_class_core: process(rstn_i, clk_i)
  861 |     variable a_pos_norm_v, a_neg_norm_v, b_pos_norm_v, b_neg_norm_v : std_ulogic;
  862 |     variable a_pos_subn_v, a_neg_subn_v, b_pos_subn_v, b_neg_subn_v : std_ulogic;
  863 |     variable a_pos_zero_v, a_neg_zero_v, b_pos_zero_v, b_neg_zero_v : std_ulogic;
  864 |     variable a_pos_inf_v,  a_neg_inf_v,  b_pos_inf_v,  b_neg_inf_v  : std_ulogic;
  865 |     variable a_snan_v,     a_qnan_v,     b_snan_v,     b_qnan_v     : std_ulogic;
  866 |   begin
  867 |     if (rstn_i = '0') then
  868 |       multiplier.res_class <= (others => '0');
  869 |     elsif rising_edge(clk_i) then
  871 |       a_pos_norm_v := fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_pos_norm_c);   b_pos_norm_v := fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_pos_norm_c);
  872 |       a_neg_norm_v := fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_neg_norm_c);   b_neg_norm_v := fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_neg_norm_c);
  873 |       a_pos_subn_v := fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_pos_denorm_c); b_pos_subn_v := fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_pos_denorm_c);
  874 |       a_neg_subn_v := fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_neg_denorm_c); b_neg_subn_v := fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_neg_denorm_c);
  875 |       a_pos_zero_v := fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_pos_zero_c);   b_pos_zero_v := fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_pos_zero_c);
  876 |       a_neg_zero_v := fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_neg_zero_c);   b_neg_zero_v := fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_neg_zero_c);
  877 |       a_pos_inf_v  := fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_pos_inf_c);    b_pos_inf_v  := fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_pos_inf_c);
  878 |       a_neg_inf_v  := fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_neg_inf_c);    b_neg_inf_v  := fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_neg_inf_c);
  879 |       a_snan_v     := fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_snan_c);       b_snan_v     := fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_snan_c);
  880 |       a_qnan_v     := fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_qnan_c);       b_qnan_v     := fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_qnan_c);
  883 |       multiplier.res_class(fp_class_pos_norm_c) <=
  884 |         (a_pos_norm_v and b_pos_norm_v) or
  885 |         (a_neg_norm_v and b_neg_norm_v);
  887 |       multiplier.res_class(fp_class_neg_norm_c) <=
  888 |         (a_pos_norm_v and b_neg_norm_v) or
  889 |         (a_neg_norm_v and b_pos_norm_v);
  893 |       if (not FPU_SUBNORMAL_SUPPORT) then
  894 |         multiplier.res_class(fp_class_pos_inf_c) <=
  895 |           (a_pos_inf_v  and b_pos_inf_v)  or
  896 |           (a_neg_inf_v  and b_neg_inf_v)  or
  897 |           (a_pos_norm_v and b_pos_inf_v)  or
  898 |           (a_pos_inf_v  and b_pos_norm_v) or
  899 |           (a_neg_norm_v and b_neg_inf_v)  or
  900 |           (a_neg_inf_v  and b_neg_norm_v);
  901 |       else
  902 |         multiplier.res_class(fp_class_pos_inf_c) <=
  903 |           (a_pos_inf_v  and b_pos_inf_v)  or
  904 |           (a_neg_inf_v  and b_neg_inf_v)  or
  905 |           (a_pos_norm_v and b_pos_inf_v)  or
  906 |           (a_pos_inf_v  and b_pos_norm_v) or
  907 |           (a_neg_norm_v and b_neg_inf_v)  or
  908 |           (a_neg_inf_v  and b_neg_norm_v) or
  909 |           (a_neg_subn_v and b_neg_inf_v)  or
  910 |           (a_neg_inf_v  and b_neg_subn_v);
  911 |       end if;
  914 |       if (not FPU_SUBNORMAL_SUPPORT) then
  915 |         multiplier.res_class(fp_class_neg_inf_c) <=
  916 |           (a_pos_inf_v  and b_neg_inf_v)  or
  917 |           (a_neg_inf_v  and b_pos_inf_v)  or
  918 |           (a_pos_norm_v and b_neg_inf_v)  or
  919 |           (a_neg_inf_v  and b_pos_norm_v) or
  920 |           (a_neg_norm_v and b_pos_inf_v)  or
  921 |           (a_pos_inf_v  and b_neg_norm_v);
  922 |       else
  923 |         multiplier.res_class(fp_class_neg_inf_c) <=
  924 |           (a_pos_inf_v  and b_neg_inf_v)  or
  925 |           (a_neg_inf_v  and b_pos_inf_v)  or
  926 |           (a_pos_norm_v and b_neg_inf_v)  or
  927 |           (a_neg_inf_v  and b_pos_norm_v) or
  928 |           (a_neg_norm_v and b_pos_inf_v)  or
  929 |           (a_pos_inf_v  and b_neg_norm_v) or
  930 |           (a_pos_subn_v and b_neg_inf_v)  or
  931 |           (a_neg_inf_v  and b_pos_subn_v) or
  932 |           (a_neg_subn_v and b_pos_inf_v)  or
  933 |           (a_pos_inf_v  and b_neg_subn_v);
  934 |       end if;
  938 |       if (not FPU_SUBNORMAL_SUPPORT) then
  939 |         multiplier.res_class(fp_class_pos_zero_c) <=
  940 |           (a_pos_zero_v and b_pos_zero_v) or
  941 |           (a_pos_zero_v and b_pos_norm_v) or
  942 |           (a_pos_subn_v and b_pos_norm_v) or
  943 |           (a_pos_zero_v and b_pos_subn_v) or
  944 |           (a_neg_zero_v and b_neg_zero_v) or
  945 |           (a_neg_zero_v and b_neg_norm_v) or
  946 |           (a_neg_subn_v and b_neg_norm_v) or
  947 |           (a_neg_zero_v and b_neg_subn_v) or
  948 |           (a_pos_norm_v and b_pos_zero_v) or
  949 |           (a_pos_norm_v and b_pos_subn_v) or
  950 |           (a_pos_subn_v and b_pos_zero_v) or
  951 |           (a_neg_norm_v and b_neg_zero_v) or
  952 |           (a_neg_norm_v and b_neg_subn_v) or
  953 |           (a_neg_subn_v and b_neg_zero_v);
  954 |       else
  955 |         multiplier.res_class(fp_class_pos_zero_c) <=
  956 |           (a_pos_zero_v and b_pos_zero_v) or
  957 |           (a_pos_zero_v and b_pos_norm_v) or
  958 |           (a_pos_zero_v and b_pos_subn_v) or
  959 |           (a_neg_zero_v and b_neg_zero_v) or
  960 |           (a_neg_zero_v and b_neg_norm_v) or
  961 |           (a_neg_zero_v and b_neg_subn_v) or
  962 |           (a_pos_norm_v and b_pos_zero_v) or
  963 |           (a_pos_subn_v and b_pos_zero_v) or
  964 |           (a_neg_norm_v and b_neg_zero_v) or
  965 |           (a_neg_subn_v and b_neg_zero_v);
  966 |       end if;
  970 |       if (not FPU_SUBNORMAL_SUPPORT) then
  971 |         multiplier.res_class(fp_class_neg_zero_c) <=
  972 |           (a_pos_zero_v and b_neg_zero_v) or
  973 |           (a_pos_zero_v and b_neg_norm_v) or
  974 |           (a_pos_subn_v and b_neg_norm_v) or
  975 |           (a_pos_zero_v and b_neg_subn_v) or
  976 |           (a_neg_zero_v and b_pos_zero_v) or
  977 |           (a_neg_zero_v and b_pos_norm_v) or
  978 |           (a_neg_subn_v and b_pos_norm_v) or
  979 |           (a_neg_zero_v and b_pos_subn_v) or
  980 |           (a_neg_norm_v and b_pos_zero_v) or
  981 |           (a_neg_norm_v and b_pos_subn_v) or
  982 |           (a_neg_subn_v and b_pos_zero_v) or
  983 |           (a_pos_norm_v and b_neg_zero_v) or
  984 |           (a_pos_norm_v and b_neg_subn_v) or
  985 |           (a_pos_subn_v and b_neg_zero_v);
  986 |       else
  987 |         multiplier.res_class(fp_class_neg_zero_c) <=
  988 |           (a_pos_zero_v and b_neg_zero_v) or
  989 |           (a_pos_zero_v and b_neg_norm_v) or
  990 |           (a_pos_zero_v and b_neg_subn_v) or
  991 |           (a_neg_zero_v and b_pos_zero_v) or
  992 |           (a_neg_zero_v and b_pos_norm_v) or
  993 |           (a_neg_zero_v and b_pos_subn_v) or
  994 |           (a_neg_norm_v and b_pos_zero_v) or
  995 |           (a_neg_subn_v and b_pos_zero_v) or
  996 |           (a_pos_norm_v and b_neg_zero_v) or
  997 |           (a_pos_subn_v and b_neg_zero_v);
  998 |       end if;
 1001 |       multiplier.res_class(fp_class_snan_c) <= (a_snan_v or b_snan_v);
 1004 |       if (not FPU_SUBNORMAL_SUPPORT) then
 1005 |         multiplier.res_class(fp_class_qnan_c) <=
 1006 |           (a_snan_v or b_snan_v) or
 1007 |           (a_qnan_v or b_qnan_v) or
 1008 |           ((a_pos_inf_v  or a_neg_inf_v)  and (b_pos_zero_v or b_neg_zero_v)) or
 1009 |           ((a_pos_zero_v or a_neg_zero_v) and (b_pos_inf_v  or b_neg_inf_v))  or
 1010 |           ((a_pos_inf_v  or a_neg_inf_v)  and (b_pos_subn_v or b_neg_subn_v)) or
 1011 |           ((a_pos_subn_v or a_neg_subn_v) and (b_pos_inf_v  or b_neg_inf_v));
 1012 |       else
 1013 |         multiplier.res_class(fp_class_qnan_c) <=
 1014 |           (a_snan_v or b_snan_v) or
 1015 |           (a_qnan_v or b_qnan_v) or
 1016 |           ((a_pos_inf_v  or a_neg_inf_v)  and (b_pos_zero_v or b_neg_zero_v)) or
 1017 |           ((a_pos_zero_v or a_neg_zero_v) and (b_pos_inf_v  or b_neg_inf_v));
 1018 |       end if;
 1021 |       multiplier.res_class(fp_class_pos_denorm_c) <= '0';
 1022 |       multiplier.res_class(fp_class_neg_denorm_c) <= '0';
 1023 |     end if;
 1024 |   end process multiplier_class_core;
 1027 |   fu_mul.result <= (others => '0');
 1028 |   fu_mul.flags  <= (others => '0');
 1033 |   adder_subtractor_core: process(rstn_i, clk_i)
 1034 |   begin
 1035 |     if (rstn_i = '0') then
 1036 |       addsub.latency            <= (others => '0');
 1037 |       addsub.exp_comp           <= (others => '0');
 1038 |       addsub.man_sreg           <= (others => '0');
 1039 |       addsub.exp_cnt            <= (others => '0');
 1040 |       addsub.man_g_ext          <= '0';
 1041 |       addsub.man_r_ext          <= '0';
 1042 |       addsub.man_s_ext          <= '0';
 1043 |       addsub.man_comp           <= '0';
 1044 |       addsub.add_stage          <= (others => '0');
 1045 |       addsub.res_sign           <= '0';
 1046 |       addsub.flags(fp_exc_nv_c) <= '0';
 1047 |     elsif rising_edge(clk_i) then
 1049 |       if (ctrl_engine.state = S_IDLE) then
 1050 |         addsub.latency <= (others => '0');
 1051 |       else
 1052 |         addsub.latency(0) <= addsub.start;
 1053 |         if (addsub.latency(0) = '1') then
 1054 |           addsub.latency(1) <= '1';
 1055 |           addsub.latency(2) <= '0';
 1056 |         elsif (addsub.exp_cnt(7 downto 0) = addsub.large_exp) then
 1057 |           addsub.latency(1) <= '0';
 1058 |           addsub.latency(2) <= addsub.latency(1) and (not addsub.latency(0));
 1059 |         end if;
 1060 |         addsub.latency(3) <= addsub.latency(2);
 1061 |         addsub.latency(4) <= addsub.latency(3);
 1062 |       end if;
 1065 |       if (unsigned(fpu_operands.rs1(30 downto 23)) < unsigned(fpu_operands.rs2(30 downto 23))) then
 1066 |         addsub.exp_comp(0) <= '1';
 1067 |       else
 1068 |         addsub.exp_comp(0) <= '0';
 1069 |       end if;
 1070 |       if (unsigned(fpu_operands.rs1(30 downto 23)) = unsigned(fpu_operands.rs2(30 downto 23))) then
 1071 |         addsub.exp_comp(1) <= '1';
 1072 |       else
 1073 |         addsub.exp_comp(1) <= '0';
 1074 |       end if;
 1077 |       if (addsub.latency(0) = '1') then
 1079 |         if (FPU_SUBNORMAL_SUPPORT) then
 1080 |           if ((fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_pos_zero_c) or fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_pos_zero_c) or
 1081 |                fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_neg_zero_c) or fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_neg_zero_c)) = '0') then
 1082 |             addsub.man_sreg <= addsub.small_man;
 1083 |           else
 1084 |             addsub.man_sreg <= (others => '0');
 1085 |           end if;
 1086 |         else
 1088 |           if ((fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_pos_zero_c)   or fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_pos_zero_c)   or
 1089 |                fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_neg_zero_c)   or fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_neg_zero_c)   or
 1090 |                fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_pos_denorm_c) or fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_pos_denorm_c) or
 1091 |                fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_neg_denorm_c) or fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_neg_denorm_c)) = '0') then
 1092 |             addsub.man_sreg <= addsub.small_man;
 1093 |           else
 1094 |             addsub.man_sreg <= (others => '0');
 1095 |           end if;
 1096 |         end if;
 1097 |         addsub.exp_cnt   <= '0' & addsub.small_exp;
 1098 |         addsub.man_g_ext <= '0';
 1099 |         addsub.man_r_ext <= '0';
 1100 |         addsub.man_s_ext <= '0';
 1101 |       elsif (addsub.exp_cnt(7 downto 0) /= addsub.large_exp) then
 1108 |         if (unsigned(addsub.large_exp(7 downto 0)) - unsigned(addsub.small_exp(7 downto 0))) > 27 then
 1109 |           addsub.man_sreg  <= (others => '0');
 1110 |           addsub.man_g_ext <= '0';
 1111 |           addsub.man_r_ext <= '0';
 1113 |           addsub.man_s_ext <= '1';
 1115 |           if (to_integer(unsigned(addsub.man_sreg)) = 0) then
 1116 |             addsub.man_s_ext <= '0';
 1117 |           end if;
 1118 |           addsub.exp_cnt(7 downto 0) <= addsub.large_exp(7 downto 0);
 1119 |         else
 1120 |           addsub.man_sreg  <= '0' & addsub.man_sreg(addsub.man_sreg'left downto 1);
 1121 |           addsub.man_g_ext <= addsub.man_sreg(0);
 1122 |           addsub.man_r_ext <= addsub.man_g_ext;
 1123 |           addsub.man_s_ext <= addsub.man_s_ext or addsub.man_r_ext;
 1124 |           addsub.exp_cnt   <= std_ulogic_vector(unsigned(addsub.exp_cnt) + 1);
 1125 |         end if;
 1126 |       end if;
 1129 |       if (unsigned(addsub.man_sreg) <= unsigned(addsub.large_man)) then
 1130 |         addsub.man_comp <= '1';
 1131 |       else
 1132 |         addsub.man_comp <= '0';
 1133 |       end if;
 1136 |       if ((ctrl_i.ir_funct12(7) xor (fpu_operands.rs1(31) xor fpu_operands.rs2(31))) = '0') then
 1137 |         addsub.add_stage <= std_ulogic_vector(unsigned('0' & addsub.man_l) + unsigned('0' & addsub.man_s));
 1138 |       else
 1139 |         addsub.add_stage <= std_ulogic_vector(unsigned('0' & addsub.man_l) - unsigned('0' & addsub.man_s));
 1140 |       end if;
 1143 |       if (ctrl_i.ir_funct12(7) = '0') then
 1144 |         if (fpu_operands.rs1(31) = fpu_operands.rs2(31)) then
 1145 |           addsub.res_sign <= fpu_operands.rs1(31);
 1146 |         else
 1148 |           if ((to_integer(unsigned(addsub.add_stage))) /= 0 ) then
 1149 |             if (addsub.exp_comp(1) = '1') then
 1150 |               addsub.res_sign <= fpu_operands.rs1(31) xor (not addsub.man_comp);
 1151 |             else
 1152 |               addsub.res_sign <= fpu_operands.rs1(31) xor addsub.exp_comp(0);
 1153 |             end if;
 1154 |           else
 1156 |             if (fpu_operands.frm = "010") then
 1157 |               addsub.res_sign <= '1';
 1158 |             else
 1159 |               addsub.res_sign <= '0';
 1160 |             end if;
 1161 |           end if;
 1162 |         end if;
 1163 |       else
 1166 |         if (fpu_operands.rs1(31) = fpu_operands.rs2(31)) then
 1167 |           if ((to_integer(unsigned(addsub.add_stage))) /= 0 ) then
 1168 |             if (addsub.exp_comp(1) = '1') then
 1169 |               addsub.res_sign <= fpu_operands.rs1(31) xor (not addsub.man_comp);
 1170 |             else
 1171 |               addsub.res_sign <= fpu_operands.rs1(31) xor addsub.exp_comp(0);
 1172 |             end if;
 1173 |           else
 1175 |             if (fpu_operands.frm = "010") then
 1176 |               addsub.res_sign <= '1';
 1177 |             else
 1178 |               addsub.res_sign <= '0';
 1179 |             end if;
 1180 |           end if;
 1181 |         else
 1182 |           addsub.res_sign <= fpu_operands.rs1(31);
 1183 |         end if;
 1184 |       end if;
 1209 |       addsub.flags(fp_exc_nv_c) <= '0';
 1210 |       if (ctrl_i.ir_funct12(7) = '0') then
 1212 |         if (((fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_pos_inf_c) and fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_neg_inf_c)) or
 1213 |              (fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_neg_inf_c) and fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_pos_inf_c))) = '1') then
 1214 |           addsub.flags(fp_exc_nv_c) <= '1';
 1215 |         end if;
 1216 |       else
 1218 |         if (((fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_pos_inf_c) and fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_pos_inf_c)) or
 1219 |              (fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_neg_inf_c) and fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_neg_inf_c))) = '1') then
 1220 |           addsub.flags(fp_exc_nv_c) <= '1';
 1221 |         end if;
 1222 |       end if;
 1223 |     end if;
 1224 |   end process adder_subtractor_core;
 1227 |   addsub.flags(fp_exc_dz_c) <= '0';
 1228 |   addsub.flags(fp_exc_of_c) <= '0';
 1229 |   addsub.flags(fp_exc_uf_c) <= '0';
 1230 |   addsub.flags(fp_exc_nx_c) <= '0';
 1233 |   addsub.small_exp <=        fpu_operands.rs1(30 downto 23)  when (addsub.exp_comp(0) = '1') else        fpu_operands.rs2(30 downto 23);
 1234 |   addsub.large_exp <=        fpu_operands.rs2(30 downto 23)  when (addsub.exp_comp(0) = '1') else        fpu_operands.rs1(30 downto 23);
 1235 |   addsub.small_man <= ('1' & fpu_operands.rs1(22 downto 0))  when (addsub.exp_comp(0) = '1') else ('1' & fpu_operands.rs2(22 downto 0));
 1236 |   addsub.large_man <= ('1' & fpu_operands.rs2(22 downto 0))  when (addsub.exp_comp(0) = '1') else ('1' & fpu_operands.rs1(22 downto 0));
 1239 |   addsub.man_s <= (addsub.man_sreg & addsub.man_g_ext & addsub.man_r_ext & addsub.man_s_ext) when (addsub.man_comp = '1') else (addsub.large_man & "000");
 1240 |   addsub.man_l <= (addsub.large_man & "000") when (addsub.man_comp = '1') else (addsub.man_sreg & addsub.man_g_ext & addsub.man_r_ext & addsub.man_s_ext);
 1243 |   addsub.start   <= fu_addsub.start;
 1244 |   addsub.done    <= addsub.latency(addsub.latency'left);
 1245 |   fu_addsub.done <= addsub.done;
 1248 |   addsub.res_sum <= addsub.add_stage(27 downto 0);
 1252 |   adder_subtractor_class_core: process(rstn_i, clk_i)
 1253 |     variable a_pos_norm_v, a_neg_norm_v, b_pos_norm_v, b_neg_norm_v : std_ulogic;
 1254 |     variable a_pos_subn_v, a_neg_subn_v, b_pos_subn_v, b_neg_subn_v : std_ulogic;
 1255 |     variable a_pos_zero_v, a_neg_zero_v, b_pos_zero_v, b_neg_zero_v : std_ulogic;
 1256 |     variable a_pos_inf_v,  a_neg_inf_v,  b_pos_inf_v,  b_neg_inf_v  : std_ulogic;
 1257 |     variable a_snan_v,     a_qnan_v,     b_snan_v,     b_qnan_v     : std_ulogic;
 1258 |   begin
 1259 |     if (rstn_i = '0') then
 1260 |       addsub.res_class <= (others => '0');
 1261 |     elsif rising_edge(clk_i) then
 1263 |       a_pos_norm_v := fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_pos_norm_c); b_pos_norm_v := fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_pos_norm_c);
 1264 |       a_neg_norm_v := fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_neg_norm_c); b_neg_norm_v := fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_neg_norm_c);
 1267 |       if (FPU_SUBNORMAL_SUPPORT) then
 1268 |         a_pos_subn_v := fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_pos_denorm_c); b_pos_subn_v := fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_pos_denorm_c);
 1269 |         a_neg_subn_v := fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_neg_denorm_c); b_neg_subn_v := fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_neg_denorm_c);
 1270 |       else
 1271 |         a_pos_subn_v := '0'; b_pos_subn_v := '0';
 1272 |         a_neg_subn_v := '0'; b_neg_subn_v := '0';
 1273 |       end if;
 1274 |       if (FPU_SUBNORMAL_SUPPORT) then
 1275 |         a_pos_zero_v := fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_pos_zero_c); b_pos_zero_v := fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_pos_zero_c);
 1276 |         a_neg_zero_v := fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_neg_zero_c); b_neg_zero_v := fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_neg_zero_c);
 1277 |       else
 1278 |         a_pos_zero_v := fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_pos_zero_c) or fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_pos_denorm_c);
 1279 |         b_pos_zero_v := fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_pos_zero_c) or fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_pos_denorm_c);
 1280 |         a_neg_zero_v := fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_neg_zero_c) or fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_neg_denorm_c);
 1281 |         b_neg_zero_v := fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_neg_zero_c) or fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_neg_denorm_c);
 1282 |       end if;
 1283 |       a_pos_inf_v := fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_pos_inf_c); b_pos_inf_v := fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_pos_inf_c);
 1284 |       a_neg_inf_v := fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_neg_inf_c); b_neg_inf_v := fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_neg_inf_c);
 1285 |       a_snan_v    := fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_snan_c);    b_snan_v    := fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_snan_c);
 1286 |       a_qnan_v    := fpu_operands.rs1_class(fp_class_qnan_c);    b_qnan_v    := fpu_operands.rs2_class(fp_class_qnan_c);
 1288 |       if (ctrl_i.ir_funct12(7) = '0') then
 1290 |         addsub.res_class(fp_class_pos_inf_c) <=
 1291 |           (a_pos_inf_v  and b_pos_inf_v)  or
 1292 |           (a_pos_inf_v  and b_pos_zero_v) or
 1293 |           (a_pos_zero_v and b_pos_inf_v)  or
 1294 |           (a_pos_inf_v  and b_neg_zero_v) or
 1295 |           (a_neg_zero_v and b_pos_inf_v)  or
 1297 |           (a_pos_inf_v  and b_pos_norm_v) or
 1298 |           (a_pos_norm_v and b_pos_inf_v)  or
 1299 |           (a_pos_inf_v  and b_pos_subn_v) or
 1300 |           (a_pos_subn_v and b_pos_inf_v)  or
 1302 |           (a_pos_inf_v  and b_neg_norm_v) or
 1303 |           (a_neg_norm_v and b_pos_inf_v)  or
 1304 |           (a_pos_inf_v  and b_neg_subn_v) or
 1305 |           (a_neg_subn_v and b_pos_inf_v);
 1307 |         addsub.res_class(fp_class_neg_inf_c) <=
 1308 |           (a_neg_inf_v  and b_neg_inf_v)  or
 1309 |           (a_neg_inf_v  and b_pos_zero_v) or
 1310 |           (a_pos_zero_v and b_neg_inf_v)  or
 1311 |           (a_neg_inf_v  and b_neg_zero_v) or
 1312 |           (a_neg_zero_v and b_neg_inf_v)  or
 1314 |           (a_neg_inf_v  and b_pos_norm_v) or
 1315 |           (a_pos_norm_v and b_neg_inf_v)  or
 1316 |           (a_neg_inf_v  and b_neg_norm_v) or
 1317 |           (a_neg_norm_v and b_neg_inf_v)  or
 1319 |           (a_neg_inf_v  and b_pos_subn_v) or
 1320 |           (a_pos_subn_v and b_neg_inf_v)  or
 1321 |           (a_neg_inf_v  and b_neg_subn_v) or
 1322 |           (a_neg_subn_v and b_neg_inf_v);
 1325 |         addsub.res_class(fp_class_pos_zero_c) <=
 1326 |           (a_pos_zero_v and b_pos_zero_v) or
 1327 |           (a_pos_zero_v and b_neg_zero_v) or
 1328 |           (a_neg_zero_v and b_pos_zero_v);
 1330 |         addsub.res_class(fp_class_neg_zero_c) <=
 1331 |           (a_neg_zero_v and b_neg_zero_v);
 1334 |         addsub.res_class(fp_class_qnan_c) <=
 1335 |           (a_snan_v    or  b_snan_v)    or
 1336 |           (a_qnan_v    or  b_qnan_v)    or
 1337 |           (a_pos_inf_v and b_neg_inf_v) or
 1338 |           (a_neg_inf_v and b_pos_inf_v);
 1340 |       else
 1342 |         addsub.res_class(fp_class_pos_inf_c) <=
 1343 |           (a_pos_inf_v  and b_neg_inf_v)  or
 1344 |           (a_pos_inf_v  and b_pos_zero_v) or
 1345 |           (a_pos_inf_v  and b_neg_zero_v) or
 1346 |           (a_pos_inf_v  and b_pos_norm_v) or
 1347 |           (a_pos_inf_v  and b_pos_subn_v) or
 1348 |           (a_pos_inf_v  and b_neg_norm_v) or
 1349 |           (a_pos_inf_v  and b_neg_subn_v) or
 1351 |           (a_pos_zero_v and b_neg_inf_v)  or
 1352 |           (a_neg_zero_v and b_neg_inf_v)  or
 1354 |           (a_pos_norm_v and b_neg_inf_v)  or
 1355 |           (a_pos_subn_v and b_neg_inf_v)  or
 1356 |           (a_neg_norm_v and b_neg_inf_v)  or
 1357 |           (a_neg_subn_v and b_neg_inf_v);
 1359 |         addsub.res_class(fp_class_neg_inf_c) <=
 1360 |           (a_neg_inf_v  and b_pos_inf_v)  or
 1361 |           (a_neg_inf_v  and b_pos_zero_v) or
 1362 |           (a_neg_inf_v  and b_neg_zero_v) or
 1363 |           (a_neg_inf_v  and b_pos_norm_v) or
 1364 |           (a_neg_inf_v  and b_pos_subn_v) or
 1365 |           (a_neg_inf_v  and b_neg_norm_v) or
 1366 |           (a_neg_inf_v  and b_neg_subn_v) or
 1368 |           (a_pos_zero_v and b_pos_inf_v)  or
 1369 |           (a_neg_zero_v and b_pos_inf_v)  or
 1371 |           (a_pos_norm_v and b_pos_inf_v)  or
 1372 |           (a_pos_subn_v and b_pos_inf_v)  or
 1373 |           (a_neg_norm_v and b_pos_inf_v)  or
 1374 |           (a_neg_subn_v and b_pos_inf_v);
 1377 |         addsub.res_class(fp_class_pos_zero_c) <=
 1378 |           (a_pos_zero_v and b_pos_zero_v) or
 1379 |           (a_pos_zero_v and b_neg_zero_v) or
 1380 |           (a_neg_zero_v and b_neg_zero_v);
 1382 |         addsub.res_class(fp_class_neg_zero_c) <=
 1383 |           (a_neg_zero_v and b_pos_zero_v);
 1386 |         addsub.res_class(fp_class_qnan_c) <=
 1387 |           (a_snan_v    or  b_snan_v)    or
 1388 |           (a_qnan_v    or  b_qnan_v)    or
 1389 |           (a_pos_inf_v and b_pos_inf_v) or
 1390 |           (a_neg_inf_v and b_neg_inf_v);
 1391 |       end if;
 1394 |       addsub.res_class(fp_class_pos_norm_c) <= (a_pos_norm_v or a_neg_norm_v) and (b_pos_norm_v or b_neg_norm_v);
 1395 |       addsub.res_class(fp_class_neg_norm_c) <= (a_pos_norm_v or a_neg_norm_v) and (b_pos_norm_v or b_neg_norm_v);
 1398 |       addsub.res_class(fp_class_snan_c) <= (a_snan_v or b_snan_v);
 1401 |       addsub.res_class(fp_class_pos_denorm_c) <= '0';
 1402 |       addsub.res_class(fp_class_neg_denorm_c) <= '0';
 1403 |     end if;
 1404 |   end process adder_subtractor_class_core;
 1407 |   fu_addsub.result <= (others => '0');
 1408 |   fu_addsub.flags  <= (others => '0');
 1417 |   normalizer_input_select: process(funct_ff, addsub, multiplier, fu_conv_i2f)
 1418 |   begin
 1419 |     case funct_ff is
 1420 |       when op_addsub_c =>
 1421 |         normalizer.mode      <= '0';
 1422 |         normalizer.sign      <= addsub.res_sign;
 1423 |         normalizer.xexp      <= addsub.exp_cnt;
 1424 |         normalizer.xmantissa(47 downto 23) <= addsub.res_sum(27 downto 3);
 1425 |         normalizer.xmantissa(22) <= addsub.res_sum(2);
 1426 |         normalizer.xmantissa(21) <= addsub.res_sum(1);
 1427 |         normalizer.xmantissa(20 downto 1) <= (others => '0');
 1428 |         normalizer.xmantissa(0) <= addsub.res_sum(0);
 1429 |         normalizer.class     <= addsub.res_class;
 1430 |         normalizer.flags_in  <= addsub.flags;
 1431 |         normalizer.start     <= addsub.done;
 1432 |       when op_mul_c =>
 1433 |         normalizer.mode      <= '0';
 1434 |         normalizer.sign      <= multiplier.sign;
 1435 |         normalizer.xexp      <= '0' & multiplier.exp_res(7 downto 0);
 1436 |         normalizer.xmantissa <= multiplier.product;
 1437 |         normalizer.class     <= multiplier.res_class;
 1438 |         normalizer.flags_in  <= multiplier.flags;
 1439 |         normalizer.start     <= multiplier.done;
 1440 |       when others =>
 1441 |         normalizer.mode      <= '1';
 1442 |         normalizer.sign      <= fu_conv_i2f.sign;
 1443 |         normalizer.xexp      <= "001111111";
 1444 |         normalizer.xmantissa <= (others => '0');
 1445 |         normalizer.class     <= (others => '0');
 1446 |         normalizer.flags_in  <= (others => '0');
 1447 |         normalizer.start     <= fu_conv_i2f.done;
 1448 |     end case;
 1449 |   end process normalizer_input_select;
 1454 |   neorv32_cpu_cp_fpu_normalizer_inst: neorv32_cpu_cp_fpu_normalizer
 1455 |   generic map (
 1457 |     FPU_SUBNORMAL_SUPPORT => FPU_SUBNORMAL_SUPPORT
 1458 |   )
 1459 |   port map (
 1461 |     clk_i      => clk_i,
 1462 |     rstn_i     => rstn_i,
 1463 |     start_i    => normalizer.start,
 1464 |     abort_i    => ctrl_i.cpu_trap,
 1465 |     rmode_i    => fpu_operands.frm,
 1466 |     funct_i    => normalizer.mode,
 1468 |     sign_i     => normalizer.sign,
 1469 |     exponent_i => normalizer.xexp,
 1470 |     mantissa_i => normalizer.xmantissa,
 1471 |     integer_i  => fu_conv_i2f.result,
 1472 |     class_i    => normalizer.class,
 1473 |     flags_i    => normalizer.flags_in,
 1475 |     result_o   => normalizer.result,
 1476 |     flags_o    => normalizer.flags_out,
 1477 |     done_o     => normalizer.done
 1478 |   );
 1487 |   output_gate: process(rstn_i, clk_i)
 1488 |   begin
 1489 |     if (rstn_i = '0') then
 1490 |       res_o  <= (others => '0');
 1491 |       fflags <= (others => '0');
 1492 |     elsif rising_edge(clk_i) then
 1493 |       res_o  <= (others => '0');
 1494 |       fflags <= (others => '0');
 1495 |       if (ctrl_engine.valid = '1') then
 1496 |         case funct_ff is
 1497 |           when op_class_c =>
 1498 |             res_o  <= fu_classify.result;
 1499 |             fflags <= fu_classify.flags;
 1500 |           when op_comp_c =>
 1501 |             res_o  <= fu_compare.result;
 1502 |             fflags <= fu_compare.flags;
 1503 |           when op_f2i_c =>
 1504 |             res_o  <= fu_conv_f2i.result;
 1505 |             fflags <= fu_conv_f2i.flags;
 1506 |           when op_sgnj_c =>
 1507 |             res_o  <= fu_sign_inject.result;
 1508 |             fflags <= fu_sign_inject.flags;
 1509 |           when op_minmax_c =>
 1510 |             res_o  <= fu_min_max.result;
 1511 |             fflags <= fu_min_max.flags;
 1512 |           when others =>
 1513 |             res_o  <= normalizer.result;
 1514 |             fflags <= normalizer.flags_out;
 1515 |         end case;
 1516 |       end if;
 1517 |     end if;
 1518 |   end process output_gate;
 1521 |   fu_core_done <= fu_compare.done or fu_classify.done or fu_sign_inject.done or fu_min_max.done or normalizer.done or fu_conv_f2i.done;
 1524 | end neorv32_cpu_cp_fpu_rtl;
 1539 | library ieee;
 1540 | use ieee.std_logic_1164.all;
 1541 | use ieee.numeric_std.all;
 1543 | library neorv32;
 1544 | use neorv32.neorv32_package.all;

 1546 | entity neorv32_cpu_cp_fpu_normalizer is
 1547 |   generic (
 1549 |     FPU_SUBNORMAL_SUPPORT : boolean := false
 1550 |   );
 1551 |   port (
 1553 |     clk_i      : in  std_ulogic;
 1554 |     rstn_i     : in  std_ulogic;
 1555 |     start_i    : in  std_ulogic;
 1556 |     abort_i    : in  std_ulogic;
 1557 |     rmode_i    : in  std_ulogic_vector(2 downto 0);
 1558 |     funct_i    : in  std_ulogic;
 1560 |     sign_i     : in  std_ulogic;
 1561 |     exponent_i : in  std_ulogic_vector(8 downto 0);
 1562 |     mantissa_i : in  std_ulogic_vector(47 downto 0);
 1563 |     integer_i  : in  std_ulogic_vector(31 downto 0);
 1564 |     class_i    : in  std_ulogic_vector(9 downto 0);
 1565 |     flags_i    : in  std_ulogic_vector(4 downto 0);
 1567 |     result_o   : out std_ulogic_vector(31 downto 0);
 1568 |     flags_o    : out std_ulogic_vector(4 downto 0);
 1569 |     done_o     : out std_ulogic
 1570 |   );
 1571 | end neorv32_cpu_cp_fpu_normalizer;

 1573 | architecture neorv32_cpu_cp_fpu_normalizer_rtl of neorv32_cpu_cp_fpu_normalizer is
 1576 |   type ctrl_engine_state_t is (S_IDLE, S_PREPARE_I2F, S_CHECK_I2F, S_PREPARE_NORM, S_PREPARE_SHIFT, S_NORMALIZE_BUSY, S_ROUND, S_CHECK, S_FINALIZE);
 1577 |   type ctrl_t is record
 1578 |     state   : ctrl_engine_state_t;
 1579 |     norm_r  : std_ulogic;
 1580 |     cnt     : std_ulogic_vector(8 downto 0);
 1581 |     cnt_pre : std_ulogic_vector(8 downto 0);
 1582 |     cnt_of  : std_ulogic;
 1583 |     cnt_uf  : std_ulogic;
 1584 |     rounded : std_ulogic;
 1585 |     res_sgn : std_ulogic;
 1586 |     res_exp : std_ulogic_vector(7 downto 0);
 1587 |     res_man : std_ulogic_vector(22 downto 0);
 1588 |     class   : std_ulogic_vector(9 downto 0);
 1589 |     flags   : std_ulogic_vector(4 downto 0);
 1590 |   end record;
 1591 |   signal ctrl : ctrl_t;
 1594 |   type sreg_t is record
 1595 |     done  : std_ulogic;
 1596 |     dir   : std_ulogic;
 1597 |     zero  : std_ulogic;
 1598 |     upper : std_ulogic_vector(31 downto 0);
 1599 |     lower : std_ulogic_vector(22 downto 0);
 1600 |     ext_g : std_ulogic;
 1601 |     ext_r : std_ulogic;
 1602 |     ext_s : std_ulogic;
 1603 |   end record;
 1604 |   signal sreg : sreg_t;
 1607 |   type round_t is record
 1608 |     en     : std_ulogic;
 1609 |     sub    : std_ulogic;
 1610 |     output : std_ulogic_vector(24 downto 0);
 1611 |   end record;
 1612 |   signal round : round_t;
 1614 | begin
 1618 |   ctrl_engine: process(rstn_i, clk_i)
 1619 |   begin
 1620 |     if (rstn_i = '0') then
 1621 |       ctrl.state   <= S_IDLE;
 1622 |       ctrl.norm_r  <= '0';
 1623 |       ctrl.cnt     <= (others => '0');
 1624 |       ctrl.cnt_pre <= (others => '0');
 1625 |       ctrl.cnt_of  <= '0';
 1626 |       ctrl.cnt_uf  <= '0';
 1627 |       ctrl.rounded <= '0';
 1628 |       ctrl.res_exp <= (others => '0');
 1629 |       ctrl.res_man <= (others => '0');
 1630 |       ctrl.res_sgn <= '0';
 1631 |       ctrl.class   <= (others => '0');
 1632 |       ctrl.flags   <= (others => '0');
 1634 |       sreg.upper   <= (others => '0');
 1635 |       sreg.lower   <= (others => '0');
 1636 |       sreg.dir     <= '0';
 1637 |       sreg.ext_g   <= '0';
 1638 |       sreg.ext_r   <= '0';
 1639 |       sreg.ext_s   <= '0';
 1641 |       done_o       <= '0';
 1642 |     elsif rising_edge(clk_i) then
 1644 |       ctrl.cnt_pre <= ctrl.cnt;
 1645 |       done_o       <= '0';
 1648 |       if ((ctrl.cnt_pre(8 downto 7) = "01") and (ctrl.cnt(8 downto 7) = "10")) then
 1649 |         ctrl.cnt_of <= '1';
 1650 |       elsif (ctrl.cnt_pre(8 downto 7) = "00") and (ctrl.cnt(8 downto 7) = "11") then
 1651 |         ctrl.cnt_uf <= '1';
 1652 |       end if;
 1655 |       case ctrl.state is
 1657 |         when S_IDLE =>
 1659 |           ctrl.norm_r  <= '0';
 1660 |           ctrl.rounded <= '0';
 1661 |           ctrl.cnt_of  <= '0';
 1662 |           ctrl.cnt_uf  <= '0';
 1664 |           if (start_i = '1') then
 1665 |             ctrl.cnt     <= exponent_i;
 1666 |             ctrl.res_sgn <= sign_i;
 1667 |             ctrl.class   <= class_i;
 1669 |             if (not FPU_SUBNORMAL_SUPPORT) then
 1670 |               if (class_i(fp_class_neg_denorm_c) = '1') then
 1671 |                 ctrl.class(fp_class_neg_denorm_c)  <= '0';
 1672 |                 ctrl.class(fp_class_neg_zero_c)    <= '1';
 1673 |               end if;
 1674 |               if (class_i(fp_class_pos_denorm_c) = '1') then
 1675 |                 ctrl.class(fp_class_pos_denorm_c)  <= '0';
 1676 |                 ctrl.class(fp_class_pos_zero_c)    <= '1';
 1677 |               end if;
 1678 |             end if;
 1679 |             ctrl.flags   <= flags_i;
 1680 |             if (funct_i = '0') then
 1681 |               ctrl.state <= S_PREPARE_NORM;
 1682 |             else
 1683 |               ctrl.state <= S_PREPARE_I2F;
 1684 |             end if;
 1685 |           end if;
 1687 |         when S_PREPARE_I2F =>
 1689 |           sreg.upper <= integer_i;
 1690 |           sreg.lower <= (others => '0');
 1691 |           sreg.ext_g <= '0';
 1692 |           sreg.ext_r <= '0';
 1693 |           sreg.ext_s <= '0';
 1694 |           sreg.dir   <= '0';
 1695 |           ctrl.state <= S_CHECK_I2F;
 1697 |         when S_CHECK_I2F =>
 1699 |           if (sreg.zero = '1') then
 1700 |             ctrl.class(fp_class_pos_zero_c) <= '1';
 1701 |             ctrl.state <= S_FINALIZE;
 1702 |           else
 1703 |             ctrl.state <= S_NORMALIZE_BUSY;
 1704 |           end if;
 1706 |         when S_PREPARE_NORM =>
 1708 |           sreg.upper(31 downto 2) <= (others => '0');
 1709 |           sreg.upper(1 downto 0)  <= mantissa_i(47 downto 46);
 1710 |           sreg.lower <= mantissa_i(45 downto 23);
 1711 |           sreg.ext_g <= mantissa_i(22);
 1712 |           sreg.ext_r <= mantissa_i(21);
 1713 |           if (or_reduce_f(mantissa_i(20 downto 0)) = '1') then
 1714 |             sreg.ext_s <= '1';
 1715 |           else
 1716 |             sreg.ext_s <= '0';
 1717 |           end if;
 1719 |           if ((ctrl.class(fp_class_snan_c)       or ctrl.class(fp_class_qnan_c)       or
 1720 |                ctrl.class(fp_class_neg_zero_c)   or ctrl.class(fp_class_pos_zero_c)   or
 1721 |                ctrl.class(fp_class_neg_denorm_c) or ctrl.class(fp_class_pos_denorm_c) or
 1722 |                ctrl.class(fp_class_neg_inf_c)    or ctrl.class(fp_class_pos_inf_c)    or
 1723 |                ctrl.flags(fp_exc_uf_c) or
 1724 |                ctrl.flags(fp_exc_of_c) or
 1725 |                ctrl.flags(fp_exc_nv_c)) = '1') then
 1726 |             ctrl.state <= S_FINALIZE;
 1732 |           elsif (unsigned(mantissa_i(47 downto 0)) = 0) then
 1733 |             ctrl.cnt <= (others => '0');
 1734 |             ctrl.state <= S_FINALIZE;
 1735 |           else
 1736 |             ctrl.state <= S_PREPARE_SHIFT;
 1737 |           end if;
 1739 |         when S_PREPARE_SHIFT =>
 1741 |           if (sreg.zero = '0') then
 1742 |             sreg.dir <= '0';
 1743 |           else
 1744 |             sreg.dir <= '1';
 1745 |           end if;
 1746 |           ctrl.state <= S_NORMALIZE_BUSY;
 1748 |         when S_NORMALIZE_BUSY =>
 1751 |           if (sreg.done = '1') or (ctrl.cnt_uf = '1') or (ctrl.cnt_of = '1') then
 1753 |             ctrl.norm_r <= '1';
 1754 |             if (ctrl.norm_r = '0') then
 1755 |               ctrl.state <= S_ROUND;
 1756 |             else
 1757 |               ctrl.state <= S_CHECK;
 1758 |             end if;
 1759 |           else
 1760 |             if (sreg.dir = '0') then
 1761 |               ctrl.cnt   <= std_ulogic_vector(unsigned(ctrl.cnt) + 1);
 1762 |               sreg.upper <= '0' & sreg.upper(sreg.upper'left downto 1);
 1763 |               sreg.lower <= sreg.upper(0) & sreg.lower(sreg.lower'left downto 1);
 1764 |               sreg.ext_g <= sreg.lower(0);
 1765 |               sreg.ext_r <= sreg.ext_g;
 1766 |               sreg.ext_s <= sreg.ext_r or sreg.ext_s;
 1767 |             else
 1768 |               ctrl.cnt   <= std_ulogic_vector(unsigned(ctrl.cnt) - 1);
 1769 |               sreg.upper <= sreg.upper(sreg.upper'left-1 downto 0) & sreg.lower(sreg.lower'left);
 1770 |               sreg.lower <= sreg.lower(sreg.lower'left-1 downto 0) & sreg.ext_g;
 1771 |               sreg.ext_g <= sreg.ext_r;
 1772 |               sreg.ext_r <= sreg.ext_s;
 1773 |               sreg.ext_s <= sreg.ext_s;
 1774 |             end if;
 1775 |           end if;
 1777 |         when S_ROUND =>
 1779 |           ctrl.rounded <= ctrl.rounded or round.en;
 1780 |           sreg.upper(31 downto 2) <= (others => '0');
 1781 |           sreg.upper(1 downto 0)  <= round.output(24 downto 23);
 1782 |           sreg.lower <= round.output(22 downto 0);
 1785 |           ctrl.flags(fp_exc_nx_c) <= sreg.ext_g or sreg.ext_r or sreg.ext_s;
 1786 |           sreg.ext_g <= '0';
 1787 |           sreg.ext_r <= '0';
 1788 |           sreg.ext_s <= '0';
 1789 |           ctrl.state <= S_PREPARE_SHIFT;
 1791 |         when S_CHECK =>
 1793 |           if (ctrl.cnt_uf = '1') then
 1794 |             ctrl.flags(fp_exc_uf_c) <= '1';
 1798 |             ctrl.flags(fp_exc_nx_c) <= '1';
 1799 |           elsif (ctrl.cnt_of = '1') then
 1800 |             ctrl.flags(fp_exc_of_c) <= '1';
 1804 |             ctrl.flags(fp_exc_nx_c) <= '1';
 1805 |           elsif (ctrl.cnt(7 downto 0) = x"00") then
 1806 |             ctrl.flags(fp_exc_uf_c) <= '1';
 1810 |             ctrl.flags(fp_exc_nx_c) <= '1';
 1811 |           elsif (ctrl.cnt(7 downto 0) = x"FF") then
 1812 |             ctrl.flags(fp_exc_of_c) <= '1';
 1816 |             ctrl.flags(fp_exc_nx_c) <= '1';
 1817 |           end if;
 1818 |           ctrl.state <= S_FINALIZE;
 1820 |         when S_FINALIZE =>
 1823 |           if (ctrl.class(fp_class_snan_c) = '1') or (ctrl.class(fp_class_qnan_c) = '1') then
 1824 |             ctrl.res_sgn <= fp_single_qnan_c(31);
 1825 |             ctrl.res_exp <= fp_single_qnan_c(30 downto 23);
 1826 |             ctrl.res_man <= fp_single_qnan_c(22 downto 0);
 1827 |           elsif (ctrl.class(fp_class_neg_inf_c) = '1') or (ctrl.class(fp_class_pos_inf_c) = '1') or
 1828 |                 (ctrl.flags(fp_exc_of_c) = '1') then
 1830 |             if ((rmode_i = "001") and (ctrl.flags(fp_exc_of_c) = '1')) then
 1831 |               ctrl.res_exp <= fp_single_pos_max_c(30 downto 23);
 1832 |               ctrl.res_man <= fp_single_pos_max_c(22 downto 0);

 1899 |   begin

 1902 |     round.sub <= '0';

 1907 |           round.en <= '0';
 1908 |         else

 1913 |           end if;

 1922 |         else

 1932 |           round.sub <= '0';
 1933 |         end if;

 1937 |         if (sreg.ext_g = '0') then
 1938 |           round.en <= '0';
 1939 |         else
 1940 |           round.en <= '1';
 1941 |         end if;
 1942 |         round.sub <= '0';
 1943 |       when others =>
 1944 |         round.en <= '0';

 1950 |   rounding_unit_add: process(round, sreg)
 1951 |     variable tmp_v : std_ulogic_vector(24 downto 0);

 1959 |       end if;
